# Gloomhaven → Frosthaven mechanic map

Every key Gloomhaven's and Frosthaven's ability cards use (ability keys, their nested keys, half-level keys), with how often each game uses it, whether Frosthaven's parser (`tools/fh_ability_keys.txt`, from the game's `AbilityData` reader) and code (`CAbility*` classes) know it, and what that means for a port. Counts are abilities (sub-abilities included), not cards. Generated by `tools/mechanics.py` from 504 Frosthaven and 608 Gloomhaven cards; the **Port** column is hand-written in `tools/mechanics_notes.py` ("(review)" = not yet judged).

Reading the status: *both games use it* = Frosthaven's own cards do this, the port is a straight copy; *FH parser + code know it* = Frosthaven kept Gloomhaven's implementation but none of its cards use it, so the mechanic must be checked in game (UI, trackers, animations) before a class relying on it ships; *UNKNOWN* = Frosthaven's reader drops the key, the plugin must implement it.

## 1. Ability verbs (what an ability does)

## 2. Ability modifiers (keys that shape a verb)

| Key | GH uses | FH uses | Status | Port | Examples |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Abilities` | 44 | 6 | both games use it | **same** — a named ability list inside `Consumes` entries (element-consume alternatives); FH uses the same shape | Berserker Final Fight bottom; Berserker Immortality bottom; Berserker Numb the Pain bottom |

| `ActiveBonus` | 167 | 283 | both games use it · **verified in game** (a ported class uses it) | **same** — `Duration` Persistent/Round shared; GH-only sub-keys listed in section 5 are all ActiveBonusData fields | BeastTyrant Blood Hunger top; BeastTyrant Concentrated Rage bottom; BeastTyrant Patch Fur top |

### Needs the plugin or a card decision

Mechanics Frosthaven's reader drops or cannot run as Gloomhaven wrote them (verdicts *plugin* and *decision* in the notes).

| Key | Verdict | GH uses | Note |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ChangeCharacterModel` | plugin | 1 | Demolitionist Mech Suit swaps to the DemolitionistMech model: CAbilityChangeCharacterModel exists but the model is a GH bundle; gh_model.py must export it and GHModel must swap at runtime (G3 item) |
| `ControlActor` | verified (with a plugin fix) | 85 | FH's control runs a list of commanded abilities (`ControlActorType: Player`). GH adds `ControlDurationType` ControlForOneAction (22: Beast Tyrant's bear, Soothsinger, Voidwarden) and ControlForOneTurn (3), both in Frosthaven's EControlDurationType; a control with NO commanded abilities (Long Con) refused every target: fixed in GHControlTurn.cs. `ControlActorType: Ally/Enemy`, `UseControllingActorPlayerControl`, `TransferAugments` unverified |
| `FilterContainer` | plugin | 4 | GH's two-filter form (`Filter1`, `Filter2`: Hatchet Executioner's Axe / Ripped From The Flesh, Summoner Living Night / Forged Ferocity): `Filter1/Filter2` appear nowhere in Frosthaven's code, the container is dropped and the ability runs unfiltered. Plugin or a single-filter rewrite per card |
| `GiveSupplyCard` | verified (with a plugin fix) | 7 | Sawbones' Medical Packs: supply cards could enter a hand and crash the load (GHSupplyCards.cs); gh_port shifts the card IDs |
| `LootData` | plugin (card fixed for now) | 1 | Mindthief Pilfer: `AdditionalLootEffect: ExtraGoldPerAdjacentEnemy`: Frosthaven's loot parser rejects the value, the card FILE fails to parse, and one failed file inside the game's parallel class loader took the whole load down (probe 2026-10-27). gh_port CARD_FIXES drops the LootData block until the plugin does the extra gold |
